### Author's acceptance of 2026-11-09 (plugin-source rules, D28 choices, D86, D87)

This part lists every change made by applying the author's acceptance of 2026-11-09 (given in Chinese; translation: "I agree to adopt them") of three groups listed in `decision-sheet.md`: (1) every rule tagged "(settled by plugin source, …; to be confirmed by the author)" — the 24 rules of (b′), from en/g4sim-binding-check.md, en/g4sim-S7-check.md and en/g4sim-S8-S9-check.md, including the k/n centre of the M1 rule (§6.7) and the two rules added by the verification of the D28 fixes and S8/S9; (2) the three choices tagged "(new choice of the D28 verification, …; to be confirmed by the author)" — (b″): joint-pattern replicas digitized through `g4sim-digi-job/1` with the once-per-scan equality check (§6.7), V_MC, V_emu and V_learn in C of the fifth item of P5 note 3 (§8.2), and ≥ 2000 repetitions in the θ-noise row of §8.7; (3) the proposals of D86 (model identity: the overlay folded into the detector-spec item, the snapshot with its event-time rule into the `response-pipeline/1` item, five items kept) and D87 (roles of conditions and alignment as θ, φ, ψ; recorded event times), including the body texts tagged "(proposed, to be confirmed by the author; D86)" or "(…; D87)".

How the acceptance is written:
- Each tag of these groups is replaced by "(accepted by the author, 2026-11-09; source en/…)", with its source citation kept: 25 plugin-source tags in the body (the 24 rules; the rule on the `source_of_facts` of exported snapshots and overlays is tagged twice, in §10.4 and in the §19 item on production use), the 3 tags of the D28-verification choices, and the 3 D86/D87 tags of §5.1 and §11.4 (D86 cited as en/g4sim-S8-S9-check.md §1.3, W1; D87 as en/g4sim-S8-S9-check.md §1.5, W2, W4). The two front-matter places that described the old tags (lines 3 and 38) are rewritten.
- Sentences that presented D86 or D87 as proposed are made unconditional (§5.1 `response-pipeline/1` row, §6.6 check of every digitization job, §11.4 Table 2, the §19 item on derived fields, §19 C27). D86 and D87 begin "**Accepted by the author (2026-11-09).**" with what was adopted, their proposed resolutions read "(adopted; …)", and their tags end "accepted by the author 2026-11-09".
- D31: its only open part was the author's confirmation of the response-layer requirement, which is one of the accepted plugin-source rules (A1/A3, extended to the digitization job by the S8/S9 job checks). The entry now says that no open part remains; its class (plugin fact) is unchanged, and it leaves the open list of line 38.

Not applied, because accepting these groups does not decide them or because they are excluded:
- D37 ("accept?"): D87 (b) binds a value with a "non-negligible uncertainty" to φ by the criterion of D37. The acceptance of D87 does not decide that criterion; D37 stays open, and D87 now says so.
- The PDE split (§19-g4c, being researched): the accepted job checks (§5.1 `response-pipeline/1` row) keep "times p only if the author adopts the PDE split of §19"; the M3 rule (§11.4, full optical tracking path) concerns the thinning of `photons.csv` rows, not the efficiency-map split. No PDE text is changed.
- §19-g6 and §19-g5 (deferred): the plugin-source rule on `source_of_facts` inside the §19 production-use item and the D86 sentence inside the §19 item on derived fields are changed; both items stay deferred, with their tags.
- D85: the accepted ≥ 2000 repetitions of the §8.7 θ-noise row do not decide its calibration; D81–D85 keep their status. D83 (7)–(19) stay upstream requests.
- Unchanged: the rules tagged "(settled by computation, …; to be confirmed by the author)" or "(a new rule, to be confirmed by the author)" (decision-sheet (b)), the 25 "accept?" items, the class-6 decisions, D68, D3, §19-14.1f, the deferred items, and the history in the earlier parts of Appendix E (which quote the old tags as they were).

### Author decision of 2026-11-10 (PDE split)

This part lists every change made by applying the author's decision of 2026-11-10 on the PDE split (§19-g4c), given in Chinese; translation: "Adopt in principle: route 2, route 3 when the PDE depends on wavelength, and a mandatory comparison with a nominal-PDE mapping run. Before writing, check the Geant4 `DielectricMetal` branch, revise the text on the four points, and then send it to independent verification." The decision rests on en/research-PDE.md (synthetic per-photon optics with g4sim 0.65.0 code downstream; Geant4 not run) and on a check of the Geant4 and g4sim sources done for this pass, en/pde-geant4-check.md. The changes were checked by three independent verifiers, each new to the work: en/pde-verify.md (four majors, ten minors, six editorial points; recheck R1–R3), en/pde-verify2.md (three majors, twelve minors, six editorial points; recheck R1–R5) and en/pde-verify3.md (three majors, ten minors, nine editorial points; recheck R1–R4). Every finding was applied, or replaced by a simpler rule where a later round showed that an earlier fix was incomplete (below), before this part was written.

How the decision is written:
- The proposed texts of en/research-PDE.md §7 are the basis. The decided texts begin "**Decided by the author (2026-11-10).**" or are tagged "(decided by the author, 2026-11-10; …)", in place of the report's "(proposed, to be confirmed by the author)"; the §14 module table, §17, C6 and C20 mention the decision as "decided by the author on 2026-11-10", as for the decision of 2026-11-09. Line 38 makes the §6.7 item, the §19 item and the tagged texts normative; the §14, §17, C6 and C20 mentions describe them.
- **Geant4 and g4sim check** (en/pde-geant4-check.md). The report re-quoted only the absorption step and the dielectric–dielectric front branch. `DielectricMetal()`, the branch of the `dielectric_metal` SiPM skins of the ZDC fixture (§15.1), was read in the Geant4 11 source (release of the view not stated) and never uses the efficiency: one uniform against reflectivity and transmittance decides absorption, and later reflections with a complex refractive index use the reflectivity computed from it. `fEfficiency` is read only in `DoAbsorption()`, except that `DielectricLUTDAVIS()` and `CoatedDielectricDielectric()` test `fEfficiency > 0` to choose absorption over transmission; the exception becomes a rejection condition in §6.7 and a request in D83 (20). g4sim v0.65.0 was read from its git tag for two points: `map-stale` compares only the materials hash, not `surfaces_sha256`, so the production run keeps the nominal sensor surfaces and this workflow checks the map's surfaces hash itself, against g4sim's hash of the mapping-run specification (finding 6; recheck R2); and, by a search for every random-number form in g4sim's C++, g4sim draws no random number when it records a detection, so the split-check pair is nested at the g4sim level (finding 7).
- **The four points of the decision**, against the report's text:
  1. Route 3 (PDE(E)) is tied to the `photons.csv` of the efficiency-1 mapping run, which must be kept and is checked against the map's counts: `efficiency-map/1` stores no energies of the detected photons, and a constant or nominal-shape scale is not allowed for a PDE(E).
  2. The split check uses the scale of the specification, 0.1σ_tot,i^design of §5.4 propagated to every θ_i via A (as D37 proposes), here as an upper confidence bound, in place of the report's 0.2 σ_tot(p̂)/p, which needs p̂ and so cannot be a plan check. It uses a pair of mapping runs with the same seed and events, at sensor efficiency 1 and at p0, whose detections are nested (first verification, major 1), so that the production map's own error, which φ_map carries, does not enter; §6.6 allows the pair's shared seed and interval (second verification, major 3). The pair is sized so that an exact split passes jointly with probability ≥ 0.94, z taken at 1 − 0.025/d for d parameters (second verification, major 1 and R2: an earlier sizing put 2 sd at the whole bound; the study configuration needs about 2 × 10⁸ detections at efficiency 1). A pair that is not nested fails the check: the second verification proposed an unpaired repeat, which the third showed to have no sd or sizing and to pass an exact split only 44–58% of the time at the paired photon number (its major 3); with finding 7, non-nesting means that something other than the efficiency changed, so it now sends the PDE to route 0. One larger pair may replace a pair that fails only through its statistical term. The check is repeated after any change of the Geant4 version, which belongs to the model identity (§5.1), or of the sensor materials or surfaces; in the mode where the map varies with θ it runs once per scan.
  3. The per-photon thinning nested in p (route 1) is tied to the case where p is θ and smooth slopes are needed. The first verification proposed the b_J item of P5 as the trigger; the third showed that b_J is not defined for the re-mapped, non-smooth predictions of route 2₁ (§8.2 note 3; its major 2). The rule is now direct: when p is θ, every readout system with an observable not linear in ε uses route 1 for all its observables (one digitization path per readout system, §6.6); for observables linear in ε the prediction μ_0 + p·(μ(1) − μ_0) has an analytic derivative and route 2₁ is used. Route 1 has a stream key of g4sim's volume form; being itself a step function of p, it is checked by σ_noise (G1) and G8, and U is built through it (third verification, recheck R1).
  4. One rule: the split is allowed only when the PDE model is exactly the sensor `EFFICIENCY(E)`, scaled or replaced; any reflectance, transmittance, refractive index, window, coating or angle dependence tied to the PDE keeps it an optical property. The split is permitted, not required: route 0 (resimulation) stays allowed, and it is the fallback when the split check fails.
- **Added or completed here** (the report gives only U(p) = p·U(1), the hashes of `photons.csv` and the PDE table, Jeffreys replicas over (channel, energy) without grid, layout or key, and route-1 keys in §6.6):
  - U(θ) for observables not linear in ε, with a surrogate model and with direct re-digitization, with one shared φ_map, and the local dimension reduction from the stacked U_j; the same for route 3 when the PDE(E) holds θ. The fixed-map definition of §6.7 says that a PDE under the split leaves the map fixed.
  - The model identity of route 3 (first verification, major 3; third verification, major 1): the PDE(E) table, without sources and with θ, φ masked, and, in the fixed-map mode only, the bytes of `photons.csv`; in the mode where the map varies with θ the `photons.csv` hashes are masked like the map hash. The digitization-job check of §5.1, kept open on 2026-11-09 pending this decision, now accepts the jobs of routes 2₁ and 3 (second verification, major 2).
  - The route-3 replicas in both modes: energy grid, Geant4's interpolation of `EFFICIENCY`, empty cells, sub-stream layout and keys, a pseudo-mass bound, a variance condition computed analytically from the Dirichlet parameters, and an exit when no grid meets them.
  - A key-distinctness check for this workflow's own streams in §6.6, the surfaces-hash check of §6.7 and `calib_contract.py`, a §8.7 row for the new design values, the phase-3 deliverables of §17, and negative tests for each new rule.
- The report's C20 wording on the linear construction agrees in substance with the author's decision of 2026-11-09 (U by digitization at the replicas; the linear construction only for observables recorded as linear in ε); the existing 2026-11-09 wording is kept.
- Corrections of the report's numbers: the pull widths of routes 2₁ and 3 are 0.98–1.02 (the 0.94–1.01 of the report's §0 is over all routes, 0.94 being route 2′ and route 0 in the map-dominated configuration); the 95% coverage of a constant scale for a PDE(E) is ≤ 0.004, not 0.
- Checked and left unchanged: the full optical tracking path of §11.4 (its accepted M3 rule already thins `photons.csv` by energy), §19-g4a, D28, D84 and D85. On D84: the efficiency-1 map holds 1/p more detections, so its replica noise is smaller, but D84 is left open. All g4sim facts cited by the new texts were re-read on hep-research v0.65.1 and on `main` at 5c2d1c7 (2026-11-10), which do not differ from v0.65.0 in `core/simulation`, `adapters/geant4/assets` or `contracts/schemas` (en/pde-geant4-check.md, finding 8); `digitize.check_job` is among them.

Nothing else is decided: D68, D3, §19-14.1f, the "accept?" items, the classes 3–6, the rules still tagged "to be confirmed by the author" (among them D37, which the new texts cite), D81–D85 and D83 itself (new items (20)–(23) are proposed upstream requests) stay as they were.
